Abstract: This paper introduces Nemo-Nemo, a practical crash-fault tolerant (CFT) consensus protocol designed to outperform existing protocols in wide-area networks by bridging design principles from the CFT and Byzantine-fault tolerant (BFT) worlds. By structuring command propagation through a causally ordered DAG, Nemo-Nemo allows all consensus replicas to propose commands with a naturally self-regulating communication regime. By exploiting multi-leader architecture, Nemo-Nemo avoids the performance bottleneck inherent to single-leader protocols. By separating command dissemination from consensus logic, Nemo-Nemo handles challenging network conditions even when consensus commits are stalled. Moreover, leader proposals that miss a deadline are never dropped, but deterministically deferred and executed later, preserving throughput under transient network delays. And by enabling Nemo-Nemo to commit on a DAG in just two network hops, it matches the latency of existing CFT systems, while achieving significantly higher throughput. The result is a robust, deployable system: the first DAG-based CFT consensus protocol proven to exceed state-of-the-art wide-area network performance in both speed and resilience.

</details>


### [140] [Watt Counts: Energy-Aware Benchmark for Sustainable LLM Inference on Heterogeneous GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09048)
*Mauricio Fadel Argerich,Jonathan Fürst,Marta Patiño-Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文构建了目前最大的LLM推理能耗开放数据集Watt Counts（5000+实验，50个模型，10种GPU），并基于数据分析提出硬件感知的部署策略，可在服务器场景降低高达70%的能耗，在批处理场景降低20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM的高能耗问题已引起社区关注，但由于缺乏能耗感知的基准测试和数据，系统运维人员在利用异构硬件的能耗权衡进行高效LLM推理部署方面缺乏指导。

Method: 创建了Watt Counts数据集，包含50个LLM在10种NVIDIA GPU上的5000多项实验数据，涵盖批处理和服务器两种场景，并提供可复现的开源基准测试工具以支持社区扩展数据集；基于此数据集进行了跨异构GPU架构的LLM推理系统级研究。

Result: 实验证明GPU选择对能效结果至关重要，最优硬件选择因模型和部署场景而异；基于数据分析的部署策略可在服务器场景下降低高达70%的能耗且对用户体验影响极小，在批处理场景下降低高达20%的能耗。

Conclusion: 硬件感知部署在异构LLM系统中具有关键重要性，Watt Counts数据集和基准测试为能效优化提供了实用指导，使从业者能够做出更优的GPU选择决策。

Abstract: While the large energy consumption of Large Language Models (LLMs) is recognized by the community, system operators lack guidance for energy-efficient LLM inference deployments that leverage energy trade-offs of heterogeneous hardware due to a lack of energy-aware benchmarks and data. In this work we address this gap with Watt Counts: the largest open-access dataset of energy consumption of LLMs, with over 5,000 experiments for 50 LLMs across 10 NVIDIA Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) in batch and server scenarios along with a reproducible, open-source benchmark that enables community submissions to expand this dataset. Leveraging this dataset, we conduct a system-level study of LLM inference across heterogeneous GPU architectures and show that GPU selection is crucial for energy efficiency outcomes and that optimal hardware choices vary significantly across models and deployment scenarios, demonstrating the critical importance of hardware-aware deployment in heterogeneous LLM systems. Guided by our data and insights, we show that practitioners can reduce energy consumption by up to 70% in server scenarios with negligible impact on user experience, and by up to 20% in batch scenarios.

</details>


### [141] [TensorHub: Scalable and Elastic Weight Transfer for LLM RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09107)
*Chenhao Ye,Huaizheng Zhang,Mingcong Han,Baoquan Zhong,Xiang Li,Qixiang Chen,Xinyi Zhang,Weidong Zhang,Kaihua Jiang,Wang Zhang,He Sun,Wencong Xiao,Andrea C. Arpaci-Dusseau,Remzi H. Arpaci-Dusseau*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Reference-Oriented Storage (ROS)，一种通过引用追踪而非物理存储来实现权重传输的新抽象，并在此基础上构建了TensorHub系统，显著降低了GPU停滞时间并提升了带宽利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型(LLM)强化学习训练需要跨异构计算资源进行高效权重传输。现有方案无法兼顾动态集群扩展的灵活性与低数据传输开销，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出引用导向存储(ROS)抽象，逻辑上呈现权重版本存储的假象，物理上不存储副本，而是追踪持有权重的GPU worker，按需直接利用这些权重提供服务。在此基础上构建TensorHub系统，集成了拓扑优化传输、强一致性与容错机制。

Result: TensorHub能够完全饱和RDMA带宽，适应多种rollout工作负载。在独立rollout中减少GPU停滞时间高达6.7倍，在弹性rollout中加速权重更新4.8倍，跨数据中心rollout停滞时间减少19倍。

Conclusion: TensorHub已部署于生产环境支持前沿RL训练，证明了ROS抽象在实现高效、灵活且低开销的权重传输方面的有效性。

Abstract: Modern LLM reinforcement learning (RL) workloads require a highly efficient weight transfer system to scale training across heterogeneous computational resources. However, existing weight transfer approaches either fail to provide flexibility for dynamically scaling clusters or incur fundamental data movement overhead, resulting in poor performance.
  We introduce Reference-Oriented Storage (ROS), a new storage abstraction for RL weight transfer that exploits the highly replicated model weights in place. ROS presents the illusion that certain versions of the model weights are stored and can be fetched on demand. Underneath, ROS does not physically store any copies of the weights; instead, it tracks the workers that hold these weights on GPUs for inference. Upon request, ROS directly uses them to serve reads. We build TensorHub, a production-quality system that extends the ROS idea with topology-optimized transfer, strong consistency, and fault tolerance. Evaluation shows that TensorHub fully saturates RDMA bandwidth and adapts to three distinct rollout workloads with minimal engineering effort. Specifically, TensorHub reduces total GPU stall time by up to 6.7x for standalone rollouts, accelerates weight update for elastic rollout by 4.8x, and cuts cross-datacenter rollout stall time by 19x. TensorHub has been deployed in production to support cutting-edge RL training.

</details>


### [142] [MATCHA: Efficient Deployment of Deep Neural Networks on Multi-Accelerator Heterogeneous Edge SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09124)
*Enrico Russo,Mohamed Amine Hamdi,Alessandro Ottaviano,Francesco Conti,Angelo Garofalo,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Maurizio Palesi,Luca Benini,Alessio Burrello*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MATCHA是一个统一的DNN部署框架，针对多异构加速器SoC生成高并发调度，在MLPerf Tiny基准上相比SOTA编译器MATCH，推理延迟降低高达35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有多个异构加速引擎的系统级芯片（SoC）上部署深度神经网络（DNN）具有挑战性，且现有的大多数部署框架无法充分利用硬件的异构性。

Method: 提出MATCHA框架，利用模式匹配、分块和映射技术实现跨硬件单元的并行执行；采用约束编程方法优化L3/L2内存分配与调度，从而生成针对并行异构加速器的高并发调度方案。

Result: 在配备两个异构加速器的SoC上运行MLPerf Tiny基准测试，MATCHA显著提升了加速器利用率，相比最先进的MATCH编译器，推理延迟降低了最高35%。

Conclusion: MATCHA有效解决了异构SoC上DNN部署的利用率不足问题，通过统一框架实现了高效的并行调度与内存优化，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Deploying DNNs on System-on-Chips (SoC) with multiple heterogeneous acceleration engines is challenging, and the majority of deployment frameworks cannot fully exploit heterogeneity. We present MATCHA, a unified DNN deployment framework that generates highly concurrent schedules for parallel, heterogeneous accelerators and uses constraint programming to optimize L3/L2 memory allocation and scheduling. Using pattern matching, tiling, and mapping across individual HW units enables parallel execution and high accelerator utilization. On the MLPerf Tiny benchmark, using a SoC with two heterogeneous accelerators, MATCHA improves accelerator utilization and reduces inference latency by up to 35% with respect to the the state-of-the-art MATCH compiler.

</details>


### [143] [Sustaining Exascale Performance: Lessons from HPL and HPL-MxP on Aurora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09517)
*Kazushige Goto,Huda Ibeid,Kalyan Kumaran,Servesh Muralidharan,Anthony-Trung Nguyen,Aditya Nishtala*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分享了在Intel Aurora百亿亿次系统上运行HPL和HPL-MxP基准测试的实战经验，通过系统级优化将FP64 HPL性能从0.585EF/s提升至1.01EF/s，HPL-MxP达到11.64EF/s，总结了在异构超大规模系统上维持持续性能的关键工程实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实生产部署约束下维持百亿亿次级的持续性能，需要跨系统层协调的工程选择和运维实践，这些经验只有在大规模实际部署中才能显现，值得系统性地识别、分类并总结，以指导未来紧耦合异构系统的设计与优化。

Method: 研究基于Aurora系统（首个大规模部署Intel独立GPU、CPU直连网络接口及最大生产级Slingshot-11互连的百亿亿次系统）开展三轮性能提升战役，运行HPL和HPL-MxP基准测试，识别并分类生产规模下维持性能的系统级工程选择，包括确定性 locality-aware 资源映射、显式CPU-GPU流水线、混合精度编排，以及大规模同步停滞后引入的混合P2P/集体通信容错策略。

Result: FP64 HPL性能从5,439节点上的0.585EF/s提升至9,234节点上的1.01EF/s；HPL-MxP达到11.64EF/s，通过混合精度运算和Intel AMX加速实现相对FP64 11.5倍的加速。系统级优化措施成功支撑了这些结果的持续实现。

Conclusion: 虽然部分观测结果具有Aurora系统特异性，但关于确定性资源映射、CPU-GPU流水线、混合精度编排及混合容错策略等更广泛的经验教训，很可能适用于极端规模下的紧耦合异构系统，为未来百亿亿次系统的工程实践提供参考。

Abstract: Sustaining exascale performance in production requires engineering choices and operational practices that emerge only under real deployment constraints and demand coordination across system layers. This paper reports experience from three successive campaigns running HPL and HPL-MxP on Aurora, an Intel-based exascale system featuring the first large-scale deployment of Intel discrete GPUs, CPU-attached network interfaces, and the largest production Slingshot-11 interconnect. Aurora progressed from 0.585EF/s on 5,439 nodes to 1.01EF/s on 9,234 nodes in FP64 HPL, while HPL-MxP reached 11.64EF/s, an 11.5x speedup over FP64 enabled by mixed-precision arithmetic and Intel AMX acceleration. We identify and classify by role at production scale the system-level choices that sustained these results, including deterministic locality-aware resource mapping, explicit CPU-GPU pipelining, mixed-precision orchestration, and a hybrid P2P/collective resilience strategy introduced after synchronization stalls at scale. While some observations are Aurora-specific, the broader lessons are likely to apply to tightly coupled heterogeneous systems at extreme scale.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Beyond the Individual: Virtualizing Multi-Disciplinary Reasoning for Clinical Intake via Collaborative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08927)
*Huangwei Chen,Wu Li,Junhao Jia,Yining Chen,Xiaotao Pang,Ya-Long Chen,Li Gonghui,Haishuai Wang,Jiajun Bu,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Aegle，一个同步虚拟多学科团队(MDT)框架，通过基于图的多智能体架构将MDT级别的推理引入门诊咨询，在文档质量、咨询能力和诊断准确性方面均优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门诊初诊咨询对临床决策至关重要，但通常由单一医师在时间压力下进行，容易产生认知偏差和证据收集不完整。虽然多学科团队(MDT)可以降低这些风险，但成本高昂且难以扩展到实时接诊。

Method: Aegle采用基于图的多智能体架构：(1)使用结构化SOAP表示形式化咨询状态，将证据收集与诊断推理分离以提高可追溯性和偏差控制；(2)编排器动态激活专家智能体进行解耦并行推理；(3)聚合器将各专家推理整合为连贯的临床记录。

Result: 在ClinicalBench和真实世界RAPID-IPN数据集（覆盖24个科室和53个指标）上的实验表明，Aegle在文档质量和咨询能力方面持续优于最先进的商业和开源模型，同时提高了最终诊断准确性。

Conclusion: Aegle成功将MDT级别的推理能力引入门诊咨询场景，在保证医疗决策质量的同时解决了传统MDT成本高、难扩展的问题，为门诊咨询提供了可扩展的智能化解决方案。

Abstract: The initial outpatient consultation is critical for clinical decision-making, yet it is often conducted by a single physician under time pressure, making it prone to cognitive biases and incomplete evidence capture. Although the Multi-Disciplinary Team (MDT) reduces these risks, they are costly and difficult to scale to real-time intake. We propose Aegle, a synchronous virtual MDT framework that brings MDT-level reasoning to outpatient consultations via a graph-based multi-agent architecture. Aegle formalizes the consultation state using a structured SOAP representation, separating evidence collection from diagnostic reasoning to improve traceability and bias control. An orchestrator dynamically activates specialist agents, which perform decoupled parallel reasoning and are subsequently integrated by an aggregator into a coherent clinical note. Experiments on ClinicalBench and a real-world RAPID-IPN dataset across 24 departments and 53 metrics show that Aegle consistently outperforms state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source models in documentation quality and consultation capability, while also improving final diagnosis accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/HovChen/Aegle.

</details>


### [145] [Aligned Agents, Biased Swarm: Measuring Bias Amplification in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08963)
*Keyu Li,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究通过基准测试发现，多智能体系统的结构复杂性往往会放大偏见而非缓解，即使单个智能体保持中立，特定结构也会导致系统性偏见加剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体系统(MAS)在复杂工作流中的广泛应用，其涌现特性——尤其是偏见的累积——仍然缺乏深入理解。由于现实世界的MAS过于复杂而难以完全分析，评估其伦理鲁棒性需要首先隔离其基础机制。

Method: 研究者引入了Discrim-Eval-Open基准，通过强制跨人口统计学群体的比较判断来绕过单个模型的中立性。研究分析了不同结构下的偏见级联效应，并剥离了高级群体复杂性以研究基础动力学。

Result: 研究发现架构复杂性往往加剧偏见而非缓解；即使隔离的智能体中立运行，也会观察到系统性放大；识别出"触发器脆弱性"现象，即注入纯客观语境会急剧加速极化。

Conclusion: 通过研究基础动力学，本研究确立了一个关键基线：结构复杂性并不保证伦理鲁棒性，这为理解和缓解多智能体系统中的偏见问题提供了重要参考。

Abstract: While Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are increasingly deployed for complex workflows, their emergent properties-particularly the accumulation of bias-remain poorly understood. Because real-world MAS are too complex to analyze entirely, evaluating their ethical robustness requires first isolating their foundational mechanics. In this work, we conduct a baseline empirical study investigating how basic MAS topologies and feedback loops influence prejudice. Contrary to the assumption that multi-agent collaboration naturally dilutes bias, we hypothesize that structured workflows act as echo chambers, amplifying minor stochastic biases into systemic polarization. To evaluate this, we introduce Discrim-Eval-Open, an open-ended benchmark that bypasses individual model neutrality through forced comparative judgments across demographic groups. Analyzing bias cascades across various structures reveals that architectural sophistication frequently exacerbates bias rather than mitigating it. We observe systemic amplification even when isolated agents operate neutrally, and identify a 'Trigger Vulnerability' where injecting purely objective context drastically accelerates polarization. By stripping away advanced swarm complexity to study foundational dynamics, we establish a crucial baseline: structural complexity does not guarantee ethical robustness. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhihao1/MAS-Bias.

</details>


### [146] [Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning for Low-Carbon P2P Energy Trading among Self-Interested Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08973)
*Junhao Ren,Honglin Gao,Lan Zhao,Qiyu Kang,Gaoxi Xiao,Yajuan Sun*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体强化学习框架，用于自利微电网在P2P电力交易中的投标策略，实现了可再生能源消纳提升、碳排放降低和经济福利增加的多赢局面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源发电和需求动态的不确定性给日前调度带来挑战。为了提高可再生能源渗透率并维持日内平衡，需要设计有效的机制来协调自利微电网之间的电力交易。

Method: 开发了一个多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架。每个微电网作为独立智能体，在时变主电网电价下，通过储能套利优化自身利润，同时提交价格和数量投标。提出了一种市场出清机制来协调交易并促进激励相容性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，学习到的投标策略提高了可再生能源利用率，减少了对高碳电力的依赖，同时增加了社区层面的经济福利。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地在减少排放和促进当地繁荣之间实现了双赢，证明了所提框架在提升可再生能源消纳和维持系统平衡方面的有效性。

Abstract: Uncertainties in renewable generation and demand dynamics challenge day-ahead scheduling. To enhance renewable penetration and maintain intra-day balance, we develop a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for self-interested microgrids participating in peer-to-peer (P2P) electricity trading. Each microgrid independently bids both price and quantity while optimizing its own profit via storage arbitrage under time-varying main-grid prices. A market-clearing mechanism coordinating trades and promoting incentive compatibility is proposed. Simulation results show that the learned bidding policy improves renewable utilization and reduces reliance on high-carbon electricity, while increasing community-level economic welfare, delivering a win-win situation in emission reduction and local prosperity.

</details>


### [147] [Social Reality Construction via Active Inference: Modeling the Dialectic of Conformity and Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09026)
*Kentaro Nomura,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于主动推理的多智能体仿真模型，在统一框架下形式化了社会现实的辩证构成过程，发现社会表征与观察分布之间存在循环互构关系，智能体通过创造性活动内生地构建了文化生态位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会智能体既能内化集体规范，又能通过创造性活动重塑这些规范，然而现有的计算模型尚未在统一框架下捕捉这种双向过程。为了填补这一空白，研究需要建立一个能够形式化社会现实辩证构成的计算模型。

Method: 提出了一个基于主动推理的多智能体仿真模型，将其部署在结构化社会网络上。每个智能体维护一个内部生成模型，通过与邻居交流形成社会先验，创造新的观察，并有选择地将他人的创造融入记忆中，从而模拟社会表征的形成与演化过程。

Result: 仿真实验揭示了三个主要发现：（1）信息凝聚的社会群体内生性涌现，其表征对齐反映了底层网络的集群拓扑结构；（2）社会表征与观察分布之间产生循环互构关系，通过智能体的创造性活动将表征结构投射到外部世界；（3）创造的传播表现出选择性、异质性模式，与社会表征的稳定扩散不同，表明智能体通过局部互动动态构建了文化生态位。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，社会顺从性与创造性偏离之间的相互作用能够导致共享社会现实的内生性形成与分化，为理解社会规范的内化与重塑这一双向过程提供了计算框架和实证支持。

Abstract: Social agents both internalize collective norms and reshape them through creative action, yet computational models have not captured this bidirectional process within a unified framework. We propose a multi-agent simulation model grounded in active inference that formalizes the dialectical constitution of social reality on a structured social network. Each agent maintains an internal generative model, communicates with neighbors to form social priors, creates novel observations, and selectively incorporates others' creations into memory. Simulation experiments demonstrate three main findings. First, informationally cohesive social groups emerge endogenously, with representational alignment mirroring the cluster topology of the underlying network. Second, a circular mutual constitution arises between social representations and the observation distribution, maintained through agents' creative acts that project representational structure onto the external world. Third, the propagation of creations exhibits selective, heterogeneous patterns distinct from the stable diffusion of social representations, indicating that agents construct cultural niches through local interaction dynamics. These results suggest that the interplay between social conformity and creative deviation can give rise to the endogenous formation and differentiation of shared social reality.

</details>


### [148] [Plasticity-Enhanced Multi-Agent Mixture of Experts for Dynamic Objective Adaptation in UAVs-Assisted Emergency Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09028)
*Wen Qiu,Zhiqiang He,Wei Zhao,Hiroshi Masui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出PE-MAMoE框架，通过稀疏门控混合专家和非参数相位控制器解决无人机基站在动态环境下的塑性损失问题，显著提升了服务质量和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机作为空中基站可在灾后快速恢复网络连接，但用户移动性和流量需求的突变会导致服务质量权衡发生变化并引发强烈的非平稳性。在这种环境变化下，深度强化学习策略会因表征崩溃和神经元休眠而出现塑性损失，严重影响适应能力。

Method: 提出塑性增强多智能体混合专家（PE-MAMoE）框架，采用集中训练与分散执行架构，基于多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）。该框架为每个UAV配备稀疏门控混合专家执行器，其路由器每步选择单一专家。设计非参数相位控制器，在相位切换后注入短暂的专家随机扰动，重置动作对数标准差，退火熵和学习率，并调度路由器温度，以在不破坏安全行为的前提下重新激活策略的塑性。

Result: 在理论层面，推导出动态遗憾界限，证明跟踪误差与环境变化和累积噪声能量相关。在包含移动用户和3GPP风格信道的相位驱动仿真器中，PE-MAMoE相比最佳基线：将标准化四分位均值回报提升26.3%，服务用户容量增加12.8%，碰撞减少约75%。诊断结果证实了专家特征秩持续较高以及在状态切换时周期性休眠神经元的恢复。

Conclusion: PE-MAMoE成功解决了无人机基站在非平稳环境下的塑性损失问题，通过混合专家架构和相位控制机制实现了高性能适应，同时保持了行为安全性，为灾后网络快速恢复提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles serving as aerial base stations can rapidly restore connectivity after disasters, yet abrupt changes in user mobility and traffic demands shift the quality of service trade-offs and induce strong non-stationarity. Deep reinforcement learning policies suffer from plasticity loss under such shifts, as representation collapse and neuron dormancy impair adaptation. We propose plasticity enhanced multi-agent mixture of experts (PE-MAMoE), a centralized training with decentralized execution framework built on multi-agent proximal policy optimization. PE-MAMoE equips each UAV with a sparsely gated mixture of experts actor whose router selects a single specialist per step. A non-parametric Phase Controller injects brief, expert-only stochastic perturbations after phase switches, resets the action log-standard-deviation, anneals entropy and learning rate, and schedules the router temperature, all to re-plasticize the policy without destabilizing safe behaviors. We derive a dynamic regret bound showing the tracking error scales with both environment variation and cumulative noise energy. In a phase-driven simulator with mobile users and 3GPP-style channels, PE-MAMoE improves normalized interquartile mean return by 26.3\% over the best baseline, increases served-user capacity by 12.8\%, and reduces collisions by approximately 75\%. Diagnostics confirm persistently higher expert feature rank and periodic dormant-neuron recovery at regime switches.

</details>


### [149] [Risk-seeking conservative policy iteration with agent-state based policies for Dec-POMDPs with guaranteed convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09495)
*Amit Sinha,Matthieu Geist,Aditya Mahajan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在有限记忆约束下求解Dec-POMDP的多项式时间算法，通过改进的目标函数和保守策略迭代获得更好的局部最优解，在基准测试中达到了接近最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解Dec-POMDP最优解是NEXP完全问题，且最优策略基于完整历史。但在计算资源有限的实际应用中，需要更紧凑的策略表示，即基于有限记忆状态（智能体状态）的策略。尽管此类策略可能不包含最优解，但在该约束类中寻找最佳策略仍具有重要的实际意义。

Method: 采用迭代最佳响应（Iterated Best Response）风格的算法框架，保证单调改进并在Dec-POMDP模型规模的多项式时间内收敛到局部最优。为获得更好的局部最优，引入改进的目标函数以激励风险寻求（risk-seeking），并结合保守策略迭代更新。

Result: 在多个Dec-POMDP基准测试中，该方法与最先进方法表现相当，在有限记忆约束下实现了接近最优的性能，同时保持多项式运行时间。实验还表明增加智能体状态数量（更大的记忆）能带来更好的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为Dec-POMDP问题中的智能体记忆约束提供了一种新颖的解决方案，在理论保证（多项式时间、单调改进）与实际性能之间取得了良好平衡，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Optimally solving decentralized decision-making problems modeled as Dec-POMDPs is known to be NEXP-complete. These optimal solutions are policies based on the entire history of observations and actions of an agent. However, some applications may require more compact policies because of limited compute capabilities, which can be modeled by considering a limited number of memory states (or agent states). While such an agent-state based policy class may not contain the optimal solution, it is still of practical interest to find the best agent-state policy within the class. We focus on an iterated best response style algorithm which guarantees monotonic improvements and convergence to a local optimum in polynomial runtime in the Dec-POMDP model size. In order to obtain a better local optimum, we use a modified objective which incentivizes risk-seeking alongside a conservative policy iteration update. Our empirical results show that our approach performs as well as state-of-the-art approaches on several benchmark Dec-POMDPs, achieving near-optimal performance while having polynomial runtime despite the limited memory. We also show that using more agent states (a larger memory) leads to greater performance. Our approach provides a novel way of incorporating memory constraints on the agents in the Dec-POMDP problem.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [GAN-Enhanced Deep Reinforcement Learning for Semantic-Aware Resource Allocation in 6G Network Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08576)
*Daniel Benniah John*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAN-DDPG框架，通过条件GAN合成流量、连续动作DDPG和语义感知奖励优化，解决了6G网络资源分配的三大局限性，在频谱效率、延迟和丢包率上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G无线网络需同时支持eMBB、mMTC和URLLC三种异构服务，但现有资源分配机制存在语义盲区浪费带宽、离散动作量化误差和训练多样性不足三大局限，无法满足严苛的性能需求。

Method: 提出GAN-DDPG框架，集成条件生成对抗网络进行流量合成以增强训练多样性，采用深度确定性策略梯度(DDPG)实现连续动作空间优化，并引入语义感知的奖励函数设计以减少冗余数据传输。

Result: 仿真实验与统计验证表明，相比基线DDPG，该方法在URLLC、eMBB和mMTC场景下频谱效率分别提升22%、20%和25%（p < 0.001），同时降低18%延迟和31%丢包率。

Conclusion: GAN-DDPG框架有效解决了6G异构服务资源分配的关键瓶颈，通过生成式数据增强、连续控制优化和语义感知机制协同作用，显著提升了网络性能指标，为6G智能资源管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks must support heterogeneous services: enhanced Mobile Broadband (eMBB) requiring 1 Tbps data rates, massive Machine-Type Communications (mMTC) supporting 10 million devices per km, and Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communications (URLLC) with 0.1-1 ms latency. Current resource allocation suffers from three limitations: (1) semantic blindness wasting 35% bandwidth on redundant data, (2) discrete action quantization, and (3) limited training diversity. This paper proposes GAN-DDPG, a Generative Adversarial Network-enhanced Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient framework integrating conditional GANs for traffic synthesis, continuous action DDPG, and semantic-aware reward optimization. Extensive simulations with statistical validation demonstrate significant improvements: 22% URLLC, 20% eMBB, 25% mMTC spectral efficiency gains (all p < 0.001) compared to baseline DDPG, with 18% latency and 31% packet loss reduction.

</details>


### [151] [eBandit: Kernel-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08791)
*Mahdi Alizadeh*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了eBandit框架，利用eBPF将ABR算法选择移入Linux内核，通过多臂老虎机算法根据实时TCP指标动态选择最优ABR策略，在合成和真实网络环境中均取得了最佳QoE表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的用户态自适应码率（ABR）算法无法直接获取关键的传输层信号（如最小RTT、瞬时传输速率），且对网络变化的响应滞后，只能在播放缓冲区受损后才做出反应，限制了算法的性能。

Method: 提出eBandit框架，利用eBPF技术将网络监测和ABR算法选择下沉至Linux内核。在sockops程序中运行轻量级epsilon-greedy多臂老虎机（MAB）算法，基于实时TCP指标计算奖励值，动态评估并选择三种ABR启发式策略。

Result: 在对抗性合成轨迹测试中，eBandit实现了$416.3 \pm 4.9$的累积QoE，比最佳静态启发式策略高出$7.2\%$；在42个真实会话测试中，其平均每片段QoE达到$1.241$，在所有策略中表现最优。

Conclusion: 证明了基于内核驻留的多臂老虎机学习方法能够有效适应异构移动网络环境，为ABR算法优化提供了新的技术路径，实现了更优的视频体验质量。

Abstract: User-space Adaptive Bitrate (ABR) algorithms cannot see the transport layer signals that matter most, such as minimum RTT and instantaneous delivery rate, and they respond to network changes only after damage has already propagated to the playout buffer. We present eBandit, a framework that relocates both network monitoring and ABR algorithm selection into the Linux kernel using eBPF. A lightweight epsilon-greedy Multi-Armed Bandit (MAB) runs inside a sockops program, evaluating three ABR heuristics against a reward derived from live TCP metrics. On an adversarial synthetic trace eBandit achieves $416.3 \pm 4.9$ cumulative QoE, outperforming the best static heuristic by $7.2\%$. On 42 real-world sessions eBandit achieves a mean QoE per chunk of $1.241$, the highest across all policies, demonstrating that kernel-resident bandit learning transfers to heterogeneous mobile conditions.

</details>


### [152] [Generative AI Agent Empowered Power Allocation for HAP Propulsion and Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09015)
*Xiaoyu Xing,Dingyi Lu,Peng Yang,Zehui Xiong,Xianbin Cao,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交互式生成AI赋能的高空平台（HAP）功率分配智能体，通过建立考虑气动干扰的精确推进功耗模型，并设计Q3E波束成形算法，解决了6G通信中推进与通信功率分配的耦合难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAP研究大多忽略推进功率或采用替代模型而忽略了机体与螺旋桨之间的气动干扰，导致通信功率预算估算错误和波束成形性能下降。由于HAP功率分配涉及空气动力学、推进系统效率和通信系统性能（QoS和EE）的多学科紧密耦合，需要一种新的解决方案。

Method: 设计了一个交互式生成AI赋能的HAP功率分配智能体。通过与AI智能体交互，开发了考虑HAP机体与螺旋桨之间气动干扰的精确推进功耗模型。在AI智能体辅助下，构建了HAP波束成形优化问题，并提出了QoS增强型能效（Q3E）波束成形算法进行求解。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提出的推进功耗模型的准确性，证明了Q3E算法在提升用户QoS和增强HAP通信系统能效方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的AI赋能方法和Q3E算法能够有效解决HAP功率分配中的多学科耦合问题，为6G HAP通信系统的设计和优化提供了新的思路和有效的技术手段。

Abstract: High altitude platforms (HAPs) are emerging as a key enabler for 6G coverage, yet limited energy must be split between propulsion and communications. Most prior HAP studies ignore propulsion power or rely on surrogates that miss hull-propeller interference, leading to misestimated communication power budgets and degraded beamforming. More importantly, HAP power allocation is intrinsically a multi-system, multidisciplinary problem in which aerodynamics, propulsion-system efficiency, and communication-system performance (quality of service (QoS) and energy efficiency (EE)) are tightly coupled.To address these challenges, this paper designs an interactive generative artificial intelligence (AI)-empowered HAP power allocation agent.By interacting with the AI agent, we develop an accurate propulsion power consumption model that takes into account the aerodynamic interference between the HAP's hull and the propeller. Assisted by the AI agent, we further formulate a HAP beamforming problem to improve user QoS and enhance the EE of the HAP communication system.This paper also proposes a QoS-enhanced energy-efficient (Q3E) beamforming algorithm to solve the formulated problem.Simulation results demonstrate the accuracy of the propulsion-power model and the effectiveness of the Q3E algorithm.

</details>


### [153] [Multimodal Large Language Model Enabled Robust Beamforming for HAP Downlink Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09017)
*Xiaoyu Xing,Peng Yang,Guoquan Tao,Dingyi Lu,Zehui Xiong,Xianbin Cao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型（VL-LLM）的波束成形框架，用于解决高空平台（HAP）姿态抖动导致的下行链路性能下降问题，实现了22.1%的用户服务比例提升和12.5%的总速率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空平台（HAP）的姿态微小变化会导致下行波束方向显著偏移，严重降低HAP下行通信性能，因此需要开发能够预测姿态变化并主动调整波束的鲁棒通信方法。

Method: 设计了一个视觉-语言大语言模型（VL-LLM），从多变量飞行遥测数据中学习以预测短期HAP姿态；设计了离线预测误差校准程序以获得预测误差上界；基于姿态预测主动更新模拟波束成形器，并提出了一种QoS驱动的波束成形与接纳方法，包含轻量级可行性强制步骤以满足瞬时发射功率和QoS要求。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所设计的VL-LLM能够准确捕捉HAP姿态变化；所提出的波束成形方法相比代表性基线实现了22.1%更高的用户服务比例和12.5%更高的总速率；测量的平均和p99总延迟分别为36.24 ms和40.13 ms，支持实际的延迟感知部署。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态LLM使能波束成形框架能够有效解决HAP姿态抖动问题，实现鲁棒的HAP下行通信，并具有实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Small changes in high altitude platform (HAP) attitude can cause significant deviations in HAP downlink beam directions, thereby severely degrading HAP downlink communication performance. In this paper, we develop a multimodal large language model (LLM) enabled beamforming framework to achieve robust HAP downlink communications.Specifically, we design a vision-language LLM (VL-LLM) that learns from multivariate flight telemetry to forecast short-term HAP attitudes under platform shaking and support delay-aware proactive beam steering.We design an offline forecast-error calibration procedure to obtain upper bounds on forecast errors and improve the reliability of proactive analog beam steering.Based on the attitude forecasts, we proactively update the analog beamformer and propose a QoS-driven beamforming and admission method with a lightweight feasibility-enforcement step to satisfy instantaneous transmit-power and QoS requirements.Simulation results indicate that the designed VL-LLM can accurately capture changes in the HAP attitude and the proposed beamforming method achieves a 22.1% higher user service ratio and a 12.5% higher sum-rate than representative baselines.The measured mean and p99 total latencies are 36.24 ms and 40.13 ms, respectively, supporting practical delay-aware deployment.

</details>


### [154] [Scrutinizing Real-life Configurations of Random Access Procedures in Cellular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09077)
*Joris Belder,Anup Bhattacharjee,Fernando Kuipers*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过实测发现运营商的随机接入配置常不匹配部署场景，导致不必要的碰撞风险；仿真表明优化配置可使碰撞减少43%（最高61%），连接延迟降低11%（最高42%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在蜂窝网络中，基站广播的随机接入配置对连接建立至关重要。理论上网络应根据部署场景优化配置以减少碰撞，但实际运营网络中配置是否合理尚不清楚，因此有必要对现网配置进行大规模测量与分析。

Method: 研究人员在三个国家的九个网络运营商处采集了112,806个蜂窝广播信息数据点，分析了随机接入配置的实际使用情况。随后利用NS-3仿真平台模拟随机接入过程，评估不同配置方案对碰撞率和连接延迟的影响。

Result: 测量发现实际配置常与部署场景不匹配，且邻近小区往往使用相同配置，导致碰撞风险不必要地升高。NS-3仿真表明，在多小区大范围场景下调整配置，碰撞次数平均减少43%（最高61%），连接延迟平均降低11%（最高42%）。

Conclusion: 研究表明运营商在随机接入配置上存在优化空间，通过简单的配置适配即可显著提升蜂窝网络性能，降低碰撞概率与连接延迟。

Abstract: In cellular networks, base stations broadcast configurations that devices use for the random access procedure, which is a vital part of the connection setup. Ideally, the network should choose configurations based on the deployment scenario to optimize radio resource management. Doing so can, for example, decrease collisions of random access messages. We captured 112,806 data points of cellular broadcast information from nine network operators across three countries and analyzed how the operators configure the random access procedure. We found that configurations often do not fit the deployment scenario, and neighboring cells often use the same configuration, causing an unnecessarily high risk of collisions and, hence, delay in the connection setup. Furthermore, we simulated the random access procedure in NS-3 and found that by varying the configurations in a large area with many cells, the number of collisions can be reduced by 43% on average and up to 61%, and the connection delay can be lowered by 11% on average and up to 42%. Our findings indicate that simple adaptations in the random access configurations can greatly improve the performance of cellular networks.

</details>


### [155] ["Take Me Home, Wi-Fi Drone": A Drone-based Wireless System for Wilderness Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09115)
*Weiying Hou,Luca Jiang-Tao Yu,Chenshu Wu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Wi2SAR系统，利用无人机和Wi-Fi信号实现野外搜救。通过模仿已知Wi-Fi网络，触发移动设备自动重连，配合Luneburg透镜增强信号方向性，实现了无基础设施支持的长距离、穿透遮挡的受害者定位。实验证明该系统高效、实用，并已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外搜救（WiSAR）是一个长期且关键的社会挑战，迫切需要创新的自动化技术解决方案。现有方案往往依赖基础设施，难以在偏远野外环境部署，因此需要一种能够长距离工作、穿透遮挡且不依赖基础设施的自动搜救系统。

Method: Wi2SAR利用现代Wi-Fi设备自动重连已知网络的特性，通过机载Wi-Fi模仿这些网络来发现受害者设备。系统包含三项创新：(1) 快速节能的设备发现机制；(2) 基于RSS信号、利用3D打印Luneburg透镜的长距离测向方法，显著放大信号强度差异并扩展工作范围；(3) 自适应无人机导航策略，高效引导无人机靠近目标。研究团队构建了端到端原型系统进行验证。

Result: 在多种移动设备和真实野外场景的实验评估中，Wi2SAR展现了高性能、高效率和高实用性，证明了其推进自主WiSAR解决方案的潜力。系统已开源以促进进一步研究和实际部署。

Conclusion: Wi2SAR成功将利用Wi-Fi重连行为的简单理念转化为实用系统，通过设备发现、增强方向检测和智能导航三项技术创新，有效解决了技术挑战，为自主野外搜救领域提供了创新的无线解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: Wilderness Search and Rescue (WiSAR) represents a longstanding and critical societal challenge, demanding innovative and automatic technological solutions. In this paper, we introduce Wi2SAR, a novel autonomous drone-based wireless system for long-range, through-occlusion WiSAR operations, without relying on existing infrastructure. Our basic insight is to leverage the automatic reconnection behavior of modern Wi-Fi devices to known networks. By mimicking these networks via on-drone Wi-Fi, Wi2SAR uniquely facilitates the discovery and localization of victims through their accompanying mobile devices. Translating this simple idea into a practical system poses substantial technical challenges. Wi2SAR overcomes these challenges via three distinct innovations: (1) a rapid and energy-efficient device discovery mechanism to discover and identify the target victim, (2) a novel RSS-only, long-range direction finding approach using a 3D-printed Luneburg Lens, amplifying the directional signal strength differences and significantly extending the operational range, and (3) an adaptive drone navigation scheme that guides the drone toward the target efficiently. We implement an end-to-end prototype and evaluate Wi2SAR across various mobile devices and real-world wilderness scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate Wi2SAR's high performance, efficiency, and practicality, highlighting its potential to advance autonomous WiSAR solutions. Wi2SAR is open-sourced at https://aiot-lab.github.io/Wi2SAR to facilitate further research and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [156] [EYWA: Elastic Load-Balancing and High-Availability Wired Virtual Network Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09322)
*Wookjae Jeong,Jungin Jung*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EYWA，一种用于IaaS云环境的可扩展虚拟网络架构，通过分布式控制平面解决了传统覆盖网络在多租户环境下的扩展性、高可用性和负载均衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的IaaS虚拟网络架构在多租户环境下存在严重的扩展性限制，传统的覆盖网络架构已成为直接瓶颈。具体挑战包括：高可用性不足、负载均衡效率低下，以及网络地址转换（SNAT/DNAT）中存在的吞吐量瓶颈和单点故障问题。

Method: EYWA采用分布式横向扩展的控制平面和数据平面架构。其核心设计包括：(1) 通过逻辑隔离的虚拟LAN支持海量租户（约2^24=16,777,216个），每个租户拥有唯一IP范围；(2) 提供无吞吐瓶颈和无单点故障的每租户公网服务；(3) 为每个租户启用具有扩展二层语义的大型IP子网。EYWA的唯一组件是运行在每个虚拟机监控程序主机上的代理，这些代理共同构成分布式控制器，可直接部署于现有云环境。

Result: 研究团队完成了EYWA的概念验证（PoC）实现，并在实验室环境中通过测量和实验验证了其有效性。该架构成功实现了大规模数据中心的高可用性、高效负载均衡和大型二层语义支持。

Conclusion: EYWA通过创新的分布式架构设计，有效解决了传统IaaS虚拟网络在多租户环境下的核心瓶颈问题，为构建大规模、高可用的云数据中心网络提供了可行的解决方案，具有良好的实际部署价值。

Abstract: Infrastructure as a Service (IaaS) in cloud environments provides compute, storage, networking, and other fundamental resources that allow consumers to deploy and run arbitrary software, including operating systems and applications. To support multi-tenant environments, IaaS leverages virtualization, but conventional overlay network architectures have become a direct cause of scalability limitations. In particular, current IaaS virtual networks face challenges in high availability and load balancing.
  To address these issues, we present EYWA, a virtual network architecture that scales to support very large data centers with high availability, efficient load balancing, and large layer-2 semantics. EYWA overcomes scalability limitations by: (1) accommodating a large number of tenants (about 2^24 = 16,777,216) through logically isolated virtual LANs with unique IP ranges, (2) providing per-tenant public network services without throughput bottlenecks or single points of failure in network address translation (SNAT/DNAT), and (3) enabling a single large IP subnet per tenant with extended layer-2 semantics.
  EYWA combines existing techniques into a distributed scale-out control and data plane. Its only component is an agent running on each hypervisor host, which collectively act as a distributed controller. As a result, EYWA can be deployed in today's multi-tenant cloud environments. We have implemented a proof-of-concept (PoC) of EYWA and evaluated its effectiveness through measurements and experiments in our lab.

</details>


### [157] [Policy-Aware Edge LLM-RAG Framework for Internet of Battlefield Things Mission Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09493)
*Om Solanki,Lopamudra Praharaj,Deepti Gupta,Maanak Gupta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向战场物联网(IoBT)任务控制的策略感知大语言模型检索增强生成框架(PA-LLM-RAG)，通过结合策略检索、本地LLM任务规划和JudgeLLM命令验证，实现了对策略违规命令的有效检测，在保证边缘部署低延迟的同时显著提升了LLM驱动系统编排的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型为意图驱动的自主信息物理系统控制提供了良好接口，但其直接应用于任务关键的战场物联网(IoBT)环境存在显著的安全性、可靠性和策略合规性顾虑，亟需一种能够在边缘部署环境下保证安全合规的LLM编排框架。

Method: 提出PA-LLM-RAG框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)轻量级检索模块，将决策建立在操作策略和遥测数据基础上；(2)本地托管的LLM用于任务规划；(3)辅助JudgeLLM用于在执行前验证用户生成的命令。通过RoboDK构建模拟IoBT环境，在包括基准操作、威胁检测、覆盖恢复、多事件协调和策略违规请求等递增复杂度的受控任务场景中评估四个开源LLM。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架能有效检测策略违规命令，同时保持适合边缘部署的低延迟响应。其中Gemma-2B表现最佳，延迟4.17秒，成功率达100%。研究发现不同模型在推理能力与响应性之间存在明显权衡，结合确定性安全机制与JudgeLLM验证显著提升了LLM驱动IoBT编排的可靠性。

Conclusion: PA-LLM-RAG框架成功解决了LLM在任务关键型IoBT环境中的安全性和可靠性挑战，证明了将确定性保障机制与LLM验证相结合的有效性。该研究为LLM在边缘部署的战场物联网任务控制中的应用提供了可行方案，同时揭示了模型推理能力与响应速度之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising interface for intent-driven control of autonomous cyber-physical systems, but their direct use in mission-critical Internet of Battlefield Things (IoBT) environments raises significant safety, reliability, and policy-compliance concerns. This paper presents a Policy-Aware Large Language Model Retrieval-Augmented Generation (referred as PA-LLM-RAG), an edge-deployed LLM orchestration framework for IoBT mission control that integrates retrieval-augmented reasoning and independent command verification. The proposed PA-LLM-RAG framework combines a lightweight retrieval module that grounds decisions in operational policies and telemetry with a locally hosted LLM for mission planning and a secondary JudgeLLM for validating user generated commands prior to execution.
  To evaluate PA-LLM-RAG, we implement a simulated IoBT environment using RoboDK and assess four open-source LLMs across controlled mission scenarios of increasing complexity, including baseline operations, threat detection, coverage recovery, multi-event coordination, and policy-violation requests. Experimental results demonstrate that the framework effectively detects policy-violating commands while maintaining low-latency response suitable for edge deployment. Gemma-2B achieving the highest overall reliability with 4.17 sec latency and 100% success rate. The findings highlight a clear tradeoff between reasoning capacity and responsiveness across models and show that combining deterministic safeguards with JudgeLLM verification significantly improves reliability in LLM-driven IoBT orchestration.

</details>
